  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "The four-tip body. At a vertex where red ports are selected, each upper torus can be compressed using its red cap; at the other vertex of the double cover, both compressions use blue caps.",
      "figure_id": "ch:grope-figure",
      "pdf_page": 33,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "ch:grope-figure",
          "exact_quote": "The four-tip body.  At a vertex where red ports are selected,\neach upper torus can be compressed using its red cap; at the other\nvertex of the double cover, both compressions use blue caps.",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[scale=.88,every node/.style={font=\\small}]\n \\node (F) at (0,0) {$F$};\n \\node (A) at (-2,1.35) {$U_a$};\n \\node (B) at (2,1.35) {$U_b$};\n \\node (r1) at (-3,2.65) {$1:R$};\n \\node (b2) at (-1,2.65) {$2:B$};\n \\node (r3) at (1,2.65) {$3:R$};\n \\node (b4) at (3,2.65) {$4:B$};\n \\draw (F)--node[left]{$a$}(A);\n \\draw (F)--node[right]{$b$}(B);\n \\draw (A)--(r1) (A)--(b2) (B)--(r3) (B)--(b4);\n \\draw (0,-.3)--(0,-.8);\n \\node at (0,-1.1) {initial boundary};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A tree-like branching diagram showing the four-tip grope body structure. At the bottom, a vertical line labeled at its base as \"initial boundary\" leads to a node labeled $F$. From $F$, two branches labeled $a$ and $b$ diverge upward to nodes $U_a$ and $U_b$, respectively. From $U_a$, two edges branch upward to tips labeled \"1 : R\" and \"2 : B\". From $U_b$, two edges branch upward to tips labeled \"3 : R\" and \"4 : B\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The four cap-sheet contributions in $g_{j}$, for one fixed opposing sheet and up to its common intersection sign. Rows index the outer parallel copies of $C_{j}$ by $ϵ$; columns index the inner cap parallels by $η$. The unused cap identifies the two group labels within each row, so that row cancels. No comparison between $h_{+}$ and $h_{−}$ is made: the paths for different rows can differ by a bottom normal circle. This is a diagram of contributions, not a projection of embedded surfaces, following the sheet calculation in .",
      "figure_id": "ch:sheet-cancellation",
      "pdf_page": 38,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "ch:sheet-cancellation",
          "exact_quote": "The four cap-sheet contributions in $g_j$, for one fixed\nopposing sheet and up to its common intersection sign.  Rows index\nthe outer parallel copies of $C_j$ by $\\epsilon$; columns index the\ninner cap parallels by $\\eta$.  The unused cap i",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1cm,y=1cm,every node/.style={font=\\small},\n contribution/.style={draw,rounded corners,minimum width=16mm,minimum height=8mm}]\n \\node at (0,2.2) {$\\eta=+1$};\n \\node at (7.2,2.2) {$\\eta=-1$};\n \\node[anchor=east] at (-1.1,1.3) {$\\epsilon=+1$};\n \\node[anchor=east] at (-1.1,0) {$\\epsilon=-1$};\n \\node[contribution] (pp) at (0,1.3) {$+h_+$};\n \\node[contribution] (pm) at (7.2,1.3) {$-h_+$};\n \\node[contribution] (mp) at (0,0) {$-h_-$};\n \\node[contribution] (mm) at (7.2,0) {$+h_-$};\n \\draw[<->] (pp)--node[above]{unused upper tip loop $=1$}(pm);\n \\draw[<->] (mp)--node[above]{unused upper tip loop $=1$}(mm);\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A $2 \\times 2$ grid diagram. Columns are labeled $\\eta = +1$ on the left and $\\eta = -1$ on the right. Rows are labeled $\\epsilon = +1$ on the top and $\\epsilon = -1$ on the bottom. In the top row, a box containing $+h_+$ is connected horizontally to a box containing $-h_+$ by a line annotated \"unused upper tip loop = 1\". In the bottom row, a box containing $-h_-$ is connected horizontally to a box containing $+h_-$ by a line annotated \"unused upper tip loop = 1\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The deficient-color contradiction on a five-cycle. Letters denote colors in which the vertex receives at most one edge. Adjacent vertices must have different letters, so the forced alternation fails when the cycle closes.",
      "figure_id": "ten:cycle-figure",
      "pdf_page": 43,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "ten:cycle-figure",
          "exact_quote": "The deficient-color contradiction on a five-cycle.\nLetters denote colors in which the vertex receives at most one edge.\nAdjacent vertices must have different letters, so the forced alternation\nfails when the cycle closes.",
          "source_object": "fig:0003"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[scale=1.05,\n  deficiency/.style={circle,draw,fill=white,minimum size=8mm,inner sep=0pt},\n  every node/.style={font=\\small}]\n \\coordinate (v0) at (90:1.65);\n \\coordinate (v1) at (18:1.65);\n \\coordinate (v2) at (-54:1.65);\n \\coordinate (v3) at (-126:1.65);\n \\coordinate (v4) at (162:1.65);\n \\foreach \\i/\\j in {0/1,1/2,2/3,3/4,4/0}{\n  \\draw[red!70!black,line width=0.8pt]\n    (v\\i) to[bend left=3] (v\\j);\n  \\draw[blue!70!black,line width=0.8pt]\n    (v\\i) to[bend right=3] (v\\j);\n }\n \\node[deficiency] at (v0) {\\(R\\)};\n \\node[deficiency] at (v1) {\\(B\\)};\n \\node[deficiency] at (v2) {\\(R\\)};\n \\node[deficiency] at (v3) {\\(B\\)};\n \\node[deficiency] at (v4) {\\(R\\)};\n \\node[anchor=south east,align=right] at (-0.9,1.35)\n    {same deficient color:\\\\impossible};\n \\node[anchor=west,align=left] at (2.15,0)\n    {each red stroke: five red edges\\\\\n     each blue stroke: five blue edges};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A five-cycle graph arranged as a regular pentagon. Vertices are circles with letter labels: the top vertex is labeled $R$, the top-left vertex is labeled $R$, the bottom-left vertex is labeled $B$, the bottom-right vertex is labeled $R$, and the top-right vertex is labeled $B$. An arc connects the top vertex $R$ and top-left vertex $R$, labeled \"same deficient color: impossible\". Edges between adjacent vertices are drawn with colored parallel strokes; a legend to the right specifies \"each red stroke: five red edges\" and \"each blue stroke: five blue edges\"."
    }
  ],
